## 6. Formation Boundary

```yaml
formation_boundary:
  allowed:
  - Bind Source, Stable ID, Record ID, correction, conflict and gap relations already present in 2D results.
  - Recalculate typed counts and authority layers.
  - Apply append-only corrections whose original and basis are preserved.
  prohibited:
  - New web search
  - New source claim
  - Generated missing entries, historical sounds or stroke geometry
  - Method support/contradiction judgment
  - Center Position or structural meaning analysis
  - Track DB or GitHub mutation
```

## 9. Source Authority Matrix

```yaml
source_authority_matrix:
- authority_class: DIRECT_PRIMARY_TEXT_LOCATION
  usable_for: Bound transcription clause and text location
  not_equivalent_to: DIRECT_FACSIMILE_OBSERVATION
- authority_class: DIRECT_FACSIMILE_OBSERVATION
  usable_for: Exact physical glyph image/coordinate
  current_count: 0
- authority_class: OFFICIAL_DICTIONARY_DETAIL
  usable_for: Stable entry identity, lexical/morpheme relation, entry-level pronunciation
- authority_class: OFFICIAL_NORM_REPORTED
  usable_for: Modern normative pronunciation/function
- authority_class: OFFICIAL_QA_REPORTED
  usable_for: Question-scoped official explanation
  not_equivalent_to: Primary manuscript observation
- authority_class: PUBLIC_TRANSCRIPTION
  usable_for: Transcribed primary text location
  not_equivalent_to: Physical facsimile
- authority_class: DICTIONARY_HISTORY_REPORTED
  usable_for: Source-reported historical snapshots
  not_equivalent_to: Direct acoustic observation
- authority_class: SCHOLARLY_RECONSTRUCTION
  usable_for: Candidate reconstruction only
  selection_state: null
- authority_class: SCHOLARLY_INTERPRETATION
  usable_for: Candidate category interpretation only
  selection_state: null
- authority_class: UNICODE_IDENTITY
  usable_for: Scalar/codepoint identity
  not_equivalent_to: Korean reading authority
- authority_class: UNICODE_ANNOTATION
  usable_for: Character annotation
  not_equivalent_to: Historical reconstruction selection
- authority_class: MECHANICAL_NORMALIZATION
  usable_for: NFD/NFC/decomposition
  not_equivalent_to: Historical glyph lineage
- authority_class: QUERY_ROUTE_ONLY
  usable_for: Reentry candidate
  not_equivalent_to: Detail entry
- authority_class: LANDING_PAGE_ONLY
  usable_for: Resource identity
  not_equivalent_to: Internal page image inspection
- authority_class: SOURCE_LOCATION_NOT_REPRODUCIBLE
  usable_for: Gap preservation only
```

  rp06_parallel_objects:
  - slot: '01'
    object_identity: Historical initial ㆁ
    gpt_y_record: RP06-OBJ-001
    gpt_z_record: RP06-01-HISTORICAL-CHOSEONG-YESIEUNG
    unicode_layer: U+3181 ↔ U+114C
    yz_decision: UNICODE_IDENTITY_VERIFIED + RECONSTRUCTION_CONFLICT
    xz_support: SOURCE_CLOSURE_PARTIAL
    canonical_state: SOURCE_SCOPE_DIFFERENCE
    boundary: Unicode annotation supports character-name layer; it does not select [ŋ] over [ɴ] as historical reconstruction.
    merged: false
    selected_lineage: null
  - slot: '02'
    object_identity: Middle Korean initial ㅇ
    gpt_y_record: RP06-OBJ-002
    gpt_z_record: RP06-02-MIDDLE-KOREAN-CHOSEONG-IEUNG
    unicode_layer: U+3147 transcription display / U+110B conjoining
    yz_decision: SOURCE_SCOPE_DIFFERENCE + MIDDLE_KOREAN_FUNCTION_GAP
    xz_support: SOURCE_CLOSURE_PARTIAL
    canonical_state: SOURCE_SCOPE_DIFFERENCE
    boundary: Y reports function-dependent possibilities; Z closes glyph/position but leaves phonetic value open.
    merged: false
    selected_lineage: null
  - slot: '03'
    object_identity: Modern orthographic onset ㅇ
    gpt_y_record: RP06-OBJ-003
    gpt_z_record: RP06-03-MODERN-CHOSEONG-IEUNG
    unicode_layer: U+3147 / U+110B
    yz_decision: CROSS_CONFIRMED
    xz_support: SOURCE_CLOSURE_CONFIRMED
    canonical_state: CROSS_CONFIRMED
    boundary: Modern orthographic placeholder with no independent consonantal sound.
    merged: false
    selected_lineage: null
  - slot: '04'
    object_identity: Modern phonetic Ø
    gpt_y_record: RP06-OBJ-004
    gpt_z_record: RP06-04-MODERN-PHONETIC-ZERO
    unicode_layer: NO_GRAPHEME
    yz_decision: CROSS_CONFIRMED_ANALYTIC_OBJECT
    xz_support: SOURCE_CLOSURE_CONFIRMED
    canonical_state: CROSS_CONFIRMED
    boundary: Source-bound abstraction; not a Hangul Character Identity.
    merged: false
    selected_lineage: null
  - slot: '05'
    object_identity: Modern coda ㅇ [ŋ]

    yz_decision: CROSS_CONFIRMED
    xz_support: SOURCE_CLOSURE_CONFIRMED

    boundary: Modern coda function remains distinct from onset ㅇ and historical ㆁ.
    merged: false
    selected_lineage: null

Relation is not merge.

## 19. Source-Scope Differences

| ID | Object | Positions | Classification | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SCOPE-001` | ㆁ phonetic layer | Y records scholarly [ŋ]/[ɴ] conflict; Z records Unicode glyph annotation. | `SOURCE_SCOPE_DIFFERENCE` | Unicode annotation is not a selector among historical reconstructions. |
| `SCOPE-002` | Middle Korean initial ㅇ | Y reports function-dependent value; Z leaves phonetic value open. | `SOURCE_SCOPE_DIFFERENCE` | Keep function-tagged evidence gap; do not collapse to contradiction. |
| `SCOPE-003` | Ø object | Y calls it researcher normalization label; Z calls it source-bound functional abstraction. | `SOURCE_SCOPE_DIFFERENCE` | Both agree it has no grapheme identity. |
| `SCOPE-004` | Direct text observation | Y marks three public-transcription observations direct; two routes are not fully reproducible. | `SOURCE_SCOPE_DIFFERENCE` | Use DIRECT_TEXT_OBSERVATION_WITH_ROUTE_GAP where applicable. |
| `SCOPE-005` | Old-Hangul encoding | Y preserves PUA glyphs; Z closes standard compatibility/conjoining Jamo identities. | `SOURCE_SCOPE_DIFFERENCE` | PUA glyph bytes and standard Unicode identities are parallel encoding layers. |
| `SCOPE-006` | Claim authority for 이르- | Y labels Q&A snapshots DICTIONARY_REPORTED. | `CORRECTION_REQUIRED` | Reclassify as OFFICIAL_QA_REPORTED. |
| `SCOPE-007` | Additional sequence URL closure | Y reports five stable URL gaps; re-entry recovers three dictionary URLs and one stable Q&A route. | `SOURCE_SCOPE_DIFFERENCE` | Update gap state without rewriting Y input. |

## Final Guard

```text
Historical transcription is not facsimile observation.
Historical glyph description is not modern stroke order.
Unicode normalization is not glyph lineage.
Private Use rendering is not standardized historical identity.

Same visual form is not same historical object.
Unicode annotation is not reconstruction selection.
Scholarly reconstruction is not direct sound.
Middle Korean initial ㅇ is not one universal function without tagged evidence.

Candidate path is not selected lineage.
Temporal sequence is not causation.
Verification is not method analysis.
Relation is not merge.
Result.Data is not Track DB.
```

## Final Guard

```text
mechanical verification is not source closure.
public transcription is not facsimile observation.
Unicode identity is not reading authority.
representative hun is not every contextual meaning.
whole-word pronunciation is not standalone morpheme pronunciation.
historical glyph is not modern stroke order.
modern orthographic rime is not historical rime.
scholarly reconstruction is not direct sound.
candidate path is not selected lineage.
single-seat presence is not cross-seat corroboration.
correction is not input deletion.
maximum-unfolded is not uncontrolled duplication.
verification is not method analysis.
relation is not merge.
Result.Data Draft is not Final Result.Data.
Result.Data is not Track DB.
```
